You hesitate as you look at him, then shake him vigorously twice. You know there's no way he's about to forget and that it's only possible he said that on purpose. So now you grit your teeth and say, "Let me ask again, do you think we're similar people?" ”

Spamton still didn't answer your question, because the owner was walking over with a bowl of ice cream, and you had to turn your Spamton around and sit on your lap. Otherwise, he wouldn't even be able to reach the bowl, and his head would just stick out of the table.

"Maybe I should enjoy the [Unlimited Ice Cream Supply] first." Spamton said. He hasn't figured out how to answer your question yet, at least not yet. In fact, he confirms that you two are similar, but perhaps in some respects, you have completely opposite thoughts and choices. For example...... When it comes to seizing someone else's [heart-shaped object], or rather their [soul], you definitely wouldn't agree with his choice. After all, to you, it's like choosing someone else as a scapegoat to avoid hurting you, and you still chose to help him. In a way, his willingness to do whatever it takes is indeed somewhat despicable. But if you choose to help him, maybe...... Is that just as despicable? He doesn't know. But at least he knows that, in his eyes, you are an angel.

You propped your face up with one hand, leaning against the table, watching Spamton scoop up the ice cream bowl with a small wooden spoon. Honestly, his eating style was quite refined at this moment, though for some reason, his speed was quite fast. In short, you just couldn't figure it out and couldn't be bothered to dwell on it.

As Spamton ate, he seriously lifted his head and glanced at you sideways. The sound was still a crackling electric crackle, mixed with unpleasant sounds, but you felt his voice was actually a bit gentler at this moment: "At least, to me, [you] are a [spiritual harbor]." "[You] are my [angel]." ”

He was trying his best to emphasize this, but you instinctively paused. You didn't know why he said that...... No, maybe you also know why he said that. Although at first it was indeed because of the game system's constraints that you had no choice but to find Spamton and stay with him, later on, you chose to stay by his side willingly, and despite some degree of deception, you chose to trust him again. For you, Spamton has indeed become a completely different existence without knowing where it started. At least for you, it holds a different meaning. Despite living in so many rented apartments before, you have never felt a sense of belonging or home. Yet precisely because there is Spamton in the rented room now, you begin to look forward to going home.

…… For Spamton, it's probably the same. If that's the case, you might have some idea how to interpret that phrase.

After all, you not only have a home but also a lover, and have learned something from him more or less...... Although that's not what you want to learn, you have subtly picked up some of his tricks in lying and sales pitch.

So, you are two similar beings...... You can't really call yourself human, because Spamton isn't really human either. It's because of similar cores that you get close. As the saying goes, you are both lukewarm beings, and perhaps you will eventually perish, but after meeting, everything changes. After all, it's because of fundamental differences that you write different possibilities along the path of intersecting destinies.

Although Spamton didn't answer your question, you still got your own answer—perhaps the answer you got was far more genuine than what others told you. So you finally smiled with relief: "Well, if that's the case, then you're also my soul's harbor...... Well, I know maybe it shouldn't be that word, but I don't know what better word to describe it. As for what you mean to me...... I can only say it's a completely different existence. ”

If that's the case, maybe you can say 'forever' too. After all, on your life journey, you'll never meet such a special person again, and no other memories will be more vivid than those months spent together or in the gaming world.

"Ah, so... maybe I can also promise you that we will always be there for each other." Of course, if one day he doesn’t want to anymore, you’ll have to use those life-saving money he tricked you out of the first time as a threat—whether it works or not is another matter, but in any case, your fates are tightly intertwined.

This time, it was Spamton who looked up at you in surprise. He exchanged a glance with you, but you just smiled and winked at him, then leaned in and kissed him on the forehead.

This left him a bit caught off guard, just staring blankly at you...... To be honest, he was wondering how to explain that sentence, but now you look like you've completely figured it out. He wasn't sure if you really understood.

So he anxiously nodded and buried his head again. After all, his face was bright red, and of course, smoke was coming out of the puppet’s head, which you could clearly see.

You didn't intend to expose him, just put the meat slices and vegetables into the freshly boiling pot, watched Spamton finish the ice cream bowl, and then ordered an ice cream cone for him. You don't think a puppet would ruin its stomach from eating ice...... Well, to be honest, maybe not.

Spamton was holding the ice cream cone with both hands, gnawing on it. His glasses were now coated with thick white mist from the hot pot smoke, but the temperature on his face had faded quite a bit from the cold cone. You covered your mouth and chuckled quietly, as if you heard it. You kept feeling like Spamton was glaring at you. Of course, when you turned your head, Spamton was just lowering his head to bite the cone, the mist on his glasses still thick.

In short, you skillfully hugged Spamton and served food to the little puppet. Both of you had a hearty meal, and because you ordered the ice cream bowl and cone, the cold and refreshing texture was really nice. Although he didn't taste much sweetness, the texture was great, so Spamton was in a good mood.

Now you ordered an ice cream as a dessert after your meal. The weather was getting warmer, and you wanted some ice too. You waved goodbye to the boss, who looked pained, then hummed an off-key tune as you walked out.

You took a bite of the popsicle. It was milk-flavored and soft, with a bit of frostbite to it, making you shiver, but the soft texture made it taste good.

So you casually handed Spamton the ice cream to his mouth. For some reason, he seemed silent for a moment, and you didn't understand why he hadn't taken a bite, so you urged him a bit: "You should eat it." ”

Finally, he bit the part you had just taken a bite from. You seemed to hear the chewing, and after a while, he replied, "Well… thank you, Miss Y/N, for the [Kangkai]."

You nodded in satisfaction, prepared to bite the popsicle yourself again, only to stop. You finally understood what he had been hesitating about… the popsicle was now covered with his saliva.

Well, you took a deep breath and bit it anyway; after all, this was the relationship you were in now, so no big deal.

But before you even finish eating the ice cream, you feel a blanket of white before your eyes, a familiar sense of detachment washes over you. You startle in your heart. You know the game system is causing trouble, but you don't know why this time is so sudden—the promised one-month holiday!

Unfortunately, you didn’t have time to think further, nor to finish the popsicle in your hand, before losing consciousness. Spamton did too.

Saturday· END

13【】

[Main Quest: Get one of them cleared and leave smoothly] Reward: One wish, and the game system will end forever.

[Side Quest: Restoring the Truth of the Accident] Reward: 100,000 gold + 100,000 Dark Coins.

You scratched your head. The system's light screen cracked even further. At this moment, the crackling sound of electricity grew louder, and the flickering light flickered as if it might explode at any moment. Those glaring white words were firmly etched into your eyes. You believe this was true for Spamton as well—because at this very moment, you were rarely together.

Taking advantage of the fact that no one else was in the room, you quickly took a look at what it looked like. A cold, dark iron table and walls. Although there were a few windows on the walls, you couldn't see outside. You were also handcuffed, probably to guard against sudden uprising. You turned to look behind you—it was the door to this closed room. You noticed a flashing red dot in the corner—probably a surveillance camera. Just as you turned your head, the door opened, and a rugged face you didn't recognize walked in, dressed in a police uniform. At this point, you clearly knew what to do next. It was basically the kind of scenario where you were the one being interrogated.

When the officer saw you turn and face the main door, his expression grew even colder. He slammed his baton the table: "Who gave you permission to turn around? Turn around now!" ”

You obediently turn around. You don't want to get hit with a baton. Unless necessary, you still don't want to get hurt.

The officer was satisfied. He pulled over a chair and sat across from you, tapping his knuckles lightly on the table twice, raising an eyebrow: "Go ahead, what exactly happened that day?" ”

You swallowed hard and belatedly realized that to you, you actually knew nothing—after all, although you had come to this game world, you had no memories of the past, and of course, you had no idea what had happened.

However, it was too late to try to pretend to have amnesia to fool the officer. After all, from the moment you entered, you hadn't disguised yourself and accepted your identity as a matter of course. Only then did you realize that you shouldn't have accepted the new environment and integrated so easily—it was all the game world playing tricks. You cursed silently in your heart, but on the surface you still looked obedient, just gently shaking your head. That confused expression wasn't an act—after all, you really didn't know anything.

"Are you talking about which day it was, officer?" So you asked.

The officer frowned, snorted coldly, and simply tossed a few photos in front of you: "Stop pretending. You were standing at the accident scene at the time, and your clothes were stained with blood. There's no way you're completely uninvolved in this case. The forensic doctor has examined you, and the blood on your body matches that of the two victims." ”

You casually glance at the photos the police officer had thrown onto the table, then your eyes widen—oh my god, if you're not mistaken, the two deceased lying on the ground in those photos are your parents, your father's face twisted with rage, and you can even imagine what he said before he died—probably expressing regret for giving birth to someone like you. Meanwhile, your mother's face showed an instinctive fear of death, probably...... She wasn't prepared to accept death right now.

You were silent for a moment, though you didn't know whether their deaths were related to you—even though you instinctively believed this case was your doing—you still looked up, blinked, and tried to put on an innocent expression, just like you had seen earlier at Spamton—well, though you might think that expression was not only innocent but also cunning, otherwise why did the police officer across from you look so disgusted?

And you just obediently said, "Yes, officer, with a little investigation, you'll know these two are my biological parents...... It's only natural that I became a suspect, but I have no reason to kill my biological parents. No matter what, they raised me, and I still remember this kindness. Filial piety is more than enough—how could I possibly kill them?" "You almost feel sick when you hear that."

After all, their attitude toward you before was indeed decent, but there was always neglect and disrespect. After all, you were never as favored as their later sons. Although there was some dissatisfaction, it was still tolerable. But when they gave up on sending you to university and instead made you go straight to work, everything changed. They started blatantly demanding money from you, and if you couldn't give them a satisfactory amount, they would insult you over the phone. Honestly, you have endured this kind of life for a long time. Sooner or later, you won't be able to hold back, and it's normal to do such things driven by anger.

But I don't know why...... You feel a bit out of place at this scene. You just want to get away from your parents, and it's not yet enough to kill them. What exactly happened? If that's the case, maybe you're just being framed by someone else?

But the police officer just sneered twice and took out a sealed plastic bag. Looking over, it was the collected evidence. It was a knife—a kitchen knife, still stained with blood. You looked at the photo—a kitchen knife was stuck in your father's chest, probably the same knife. The mother's abdomen was cut wide, making it look like she died from excessive blood loss. That's why her face showed a fear of death...... Feeling her body grow cold and her consciousness fading, not everyone can endure this and have the willpower to do so. Facing the fear of death is probably the most instinctive kind of fear in humans.

You stared at the evidence in silence for a moment, then the police officer took out several autopsy reports and spread them out on the table. He asked coldly, "The autopsy report shows your mother, that is, Mr. X, died from excessive blood loss, with only an external wound to the abdomen. Your parents, Mr. Y, died from fatal wounds to the chest, and the kitchen knife stabbed his heart. In addition, both of them had the same drug ingredient. Since this drug is unheard of, the forensic doctors are still conducting tests. ”

You stared blankly at the autopsy report and the kitchen knife. Honestly, you thought it would be better to just confess now. After all, the evidence was so solid, it would be false to say you knew nothing...... But if you were the one committing the crime, at least you would hide this overly conspicuous evidence, which would only delay for a while. If you were still caught then, you'd have to accept your bad luck. But now, everything was so conspicuous, maybe to cover something up, like...... To cover up the drug in their bodies.

However, the main quest requires someone to clear their name...... You don't know what's really going on with Spamton at the moment, so maybe you should confess to all the crimes, since doing so might help you complete the main quest. As for the side quests, you always get a feeling that they don't make sense...... What other truth could there be?

But before that, you still have some doubts: "So, does this kitchen knife bear my fingerprints?" "With such obvious evidence, you probably wouldn't keep it, especially since the police didn't show it, so you have no choice but to take a gamble."

As it turned out, you bet right. The officer's expression stiffened, and he snorted coldly: "Yes, indeed, no fingerprints were found. There was nothing left on the knife handle, and even the bloodstains were streaked. We have reason to suspect that the suspect, you, was wrapped in a protective device like a glove to hold the knife, then stabbed it into Mr. Y's chest." ”

You sighed. Honestly, remembering nothing was the biggest mistake in this situation; otherwise, you could try to refute something. And now, you looked at the photos on the table. Besides the scene of the crime, you also saw the surroundings of the crime scene and saw a river not far away. At the same time, you glanced at the autopsy report. The body was sent at 5 p.m., and it was estimated to be four to six hours ago, which was roughly lunchtime.

Your eyes darted around, and unexpectedly, you came up with a good idea. Then you smiled and said, "Yes, yes, officer, I killed the people. I killed both of them. I never thought I could escape legal judgment. From the moment I killed them, I was determined to die." ”

The officer probably didn't expect you to confess so easily. His eyes widened slightly. You guessed that before you entered this game, "you" absolutely refused to give up. You know, this is the world of light. Perhaps all of this will be your ultimate fate there. You just can't figure out why, if that's the case, this matter would drag Spamton into it again.

Then the police came back to their senses and slammed the table hard. You naturally fell silent, just staring at him with a terrified expression. After clearing his throat, he said with authority, "I'm not asking you to confess your crimes. You should ask the priest to do these things. What we need to do now is record your confession and explain the details of your crime." ”

You feel a bit helpless, and you don't know how the police are dragging their feet. As long as they throw you in court and present the evidence, you'll admit guilt. Why is the process so complicated now? Maybe it's because you never thought about being a police officer, and naturally haven't understood how the law works.

But at this point, it's quite difficult to make up a complete story based on these fragmented contents. So you stay silent for a long time, while the police officer leans on the table and watches you. You can feel him staring at you intently, and you feel a bit awkward.

After much effort, you come up with a logically flawless story. You cough twice to signal that you are about to start recounting your "crime process." The officer clapps his hands, and you hear the door behind you creak open. You look a bit surprised as a recorder sits in another empty seat across the table.

Then the officer nodded, signaling that you could speak now. So you took a deep breath and began to tell the story you had just compiled: "...... At that time, I rarely went back to my parents' home because they kept calling to scold me and ask for money. I really couldn't hold back. The real purpose of this trip was to put a little medicine in those dishes, to make them feel pain for a while, so they would remember the lesson and stop bothering me again. ”

The recorder was writing swiftly on paper. You wondered if his writing speed could really keep up with your speaking speed. The officer crossed his arms, still staring at you, probably afraid you'd lose his temper at this moment. You couldn't help but complain inwardly—only a fool would attack a police officer at a time like this. You're just a bit a bit unsmart—you're not a fool.

"That drug is just an ordinary sleeping pill. I just wanted to teach them a lesson......" You remembered earlier that you couldn't find out the ingredients of this drug, so you added another sentence. After all, you had to make up the story look more perfect. But for some reason, the recorder suddenly chuckled, making you hesitate and glance at him again.

The officer just nodded and glanced at the recorder, telling him not to speak further. The recorder immediately lowered his head and fell silent. The officer signaled for you to continue. You took a deep breath, trying to stay calm and continue telling the story. After all, when you made it up earlier, you knew your parents would definitely do that. So you couldn't help but feel anger, your hands trembling, and your body trembling. You forced a smile, a hint of madness, and said, "I drugged you, I brought the food to the table, but to my surprise, at the table, my father insulted me again and this time told me to hand over the money face to face." My mother, on the other hand, was my father's accomplice. She joined in insulting me, but played the good cop on the side, urging me to take out money to honor them as the true act of filial piety. I couldn't take it anymore. When I went home to visit them and prepare lunch, I was already furious, and now I was even more fired up. ”

At this point, you pause again, because you realize that everything now really feels like a made-up story rather than a testimony. You regret it a little. If you'd known you'd chosen another way to tell your story, this one definitely wouldn't have passed. The recorder stopped writing, looked up at you, and gave him an awkward smile. The officer raised his hand, signaling you to continue.

…… Since he thinks there's no problem, you can keep talking for now. After all, no matter how bad things are, they can't get worse than the current situation.

"So I picked up the kitchen knife. My mother tried to stop me. Maybe she realized what I was trying to do, and at first, I didn't intend to hurt them. After all, I just wanted to teach them a lesson, so I only left a moderate wound on her abdomen while dodging her attack, and it was bleeding." You paused, looking at the police officer who seemed unresponsive, and then continued speaking.

“…… Then, seeing this, my father panicked, scolded me, cursed at me, and tried to snatch the kitchen knife from my hand. Perhaps in a fit of rage, I slashed straight at my father's chest, the knife piercing straight into his chest, and then he collapsed to the ground...... My mother was terrified too, collapsed to the ground, unable to move, and blood splattered onto my clothes. I stared blankly at the scene, trying to erase some evidence, so I hurriedly took off my gloves and threw them into a nearby river. But I didn't run any farther before being caught by the police. ”

Finally completing the entire story, you breathed a slight sigh of relief in your heart. You have to understand, you had to put in a lot of effort to tell such a story. Normally, you wouldn't have come up with a story that at first glance seemed logically sound.

But the officer just frowned, tapped his fingers on the table, signaling the recorder to stop. You quieted down, and in the silence, for a moment, you could only hear your own heartbeat—pounding like a drum. You were nervous, not knowing what else he wanted to say, which is why he ordered you to be quiet.

But the officer just lowered his head; you couldn't understand his expression, maybe it was just pure ...... Not very clear? But you clearly heard his voice, one tinged with mockery and pity. You didn't know where his pity came from, nor did you have time to think—you could only hear his voice.

He said, "I'm lying. Actually, you turned yourself in. You asked the police to send someone to take you away, but you refused to tell us the details of the case, so we had to question you." ”

…… So that's why you didn't go to court in the first place? You're a bit dazed.

"I thought you'd give a real testimony today, but it turns out you're lying...... Stay here for now." The officer pressed his brow, looking a bit tired. Then, you heard the door creak open behind you, and a police officer came in and roughly pulled you up. You quietly followed behind him, and then...... You arrived at a detention cell, where you were locked up.

To be honest, the environment in the detention room is actually pretty good, at least better than your rental apartment...... The downside is that the detention room actually has bunk beds, and you don't know who will be living with you.

Just as you were looking around, you heard a familiar voice: "Very happy to see you again." Although he said he was happy, his voice always sounded a bit gloomy.

You looked toward the sound, looked up, and spotted a little puppet sitting on the edge of the bed, swinging its legs. To be honest, you were a bit surprised, since you had been worrying about how to see him in the detention cell, so you greeted him happily: "Spamton!" ”

Then you finally notice the clothes he was trying hard to hide...... If you hadn't stood on tiptoe when greeting him, you probably wouldn't have noticed him, but you would have noticed he was wearing a blue princess dress, which was probably the source of his gloom.

Anyway, you fell silent, twitched your lips, and pointed at Spamton: "...... Why are you wearing a princess dress? ”

His voice now carried a tone of frustration and anger: "I didn't [agree to all terms and security conditions], but maybe to [share] private intimate moments with [you], I [forced] agreed to [registration terms and security matters]." When I [woke up] in this world, I was already dressed like this. ”

…… You twitched your lips again, helplessly rubbing your forehead: "Alright, maybe that's just some kind of twisted humor from some game system...... But since we're in the same room, at least we have to dress you in a set of clothes so you don't look so obvious at first glance. Although I'm curious how they actually completed the investigation process. ”

As he spoke, Spamton jumped off the bed. You had no idea what he was up to, and even if the puppet fell from that height, it wouldn't be damaged. In any case, you caught him and held him in your arms. And in your embrace, Spamton's previously tense body relaxed.

Well, it seems he just wants you to give him a hug. You shrugged and just sat down on the lower bunk holding him. Honestly, you actually don't know where to start right now.

But speaking of which, you just took a moment to glance at the game's light screen. Although it did look like it was about to shatter, it still gave you feedback on the current state of the game. Of course, the main quest is still at 0% at this stage, but the side quest has suddenly dropped to 35%. Spamton continued, but your brows furrowed. This was completely different from the evidence you saw. It seemed they used different evidence to interrogate you to prevent your collusion.

Spamton was silent for a moment before saying, "As far as I know, [Gloves: Original Location] is [Navigation: Right side of the table], not [Navigation: Rushing River]. ”

"What they probably need is [real intelligence], which is why they [present contrary evidence] to lure us into [providing relevant story content]."

"Yeah, for them, that's just a story...... I wasted all my effort and even made up such a perfect story for them. By the way, do you have fingerprints on your gloves? "You just sank for a moment and quickly pulled yourself together. After all, you still have to find a way to get at least one person out of prison. Getting both out of prison or something...... "[Gloves: fingerprint evidence] is indeed [indisputable], but ......" Spamton dragged out his voice as if hesitating, and you couldn't help but hesitate, urging, "But what?" ”

Besides, you haven't registered in the Light World, so how do they know those are your fingerprints?" ”

Although you don't know what's going on in this world now, you can still be sure of this, since Spamton is a Dark Folk.

"...... [System Entry] does contain my [fingerprint content]." His tone was a bit confused; clearly, he hadn't figured it out either.

You really have a wild imagination. Even though it probably doesn't fit logically, you still said, "Hmm...... Alright, then I roughly get it. Maybe this world is actually another timeline, where you and I are both citizens of Light...... But before you could finish, Spamton interrupted you.

At that time, you hadn't suffered what happened afterward, so of course you couldn't be an invisible person without an identity in the world of light. ”

“…… And [Medicinal ingredient: potassium cyanide]. " he said.

"I told the [Officer] that first, [Medication: Potassium cyanide] was my [Delicious Seasoning] in the food. Then, the [Hurt Guide] was that I couldn't bear to see [you] suffer [cruel treatment], so I wrapped the [knife handle] with [gloves]. The first strike only injured your mother's abdomen due to the height issue. The second stab was because your father was pushed down by you, so I inserted the [kitchen knife] into the chest." He briefly recounted the testimony from that time. Although the police and the recorder showed little reaction after hearing it, they simply walked out and left the interrogation room alone, and only about an hour later did they take him away and lock him back in the detention cell.

"So I boldly speculate: [You] will have [the time of the interrogation] after me. And [Mr. Police] acted this way to ensure our [testimony system] is [accurate]. "He added another sentence after that.

You nodded, indicating you probably understood...... Well, it's not okay not to understand, since the main quest is still at 0%. You can't think of any good way to escape, and strictly speaking, this isn't really a horror game—it's more like a kind of ...... A time-travel experiment? You know very well, you have a feeling—if it weren't for the events in the Dark World, if there hadn't been the game system...... Right now, you're just thinking about getting away from your parents, but there's no guarantee that with nothing and under their oppression, you won't do such things. Maybe this is just a crime committed by your future self in another timeline, and as for Spamton, she's probably just dragged into it for no reason.

You fell into deep thought, thinking about this while still thinking about how to revise your testimony, when suddenly Spamton spoke: "I know very clearly, that [drug ingredient: potassium cyanide] was the one I put in." ”

You shivered and instinctively opened the game screen to check. The side quest had just been at 40%, but now it had dropped to 50%. Spamton was right. You realized this, but doubt quickly crept up in your heart.

"But if that's the case...... Why did you appear in the world of light with me, and commit a crime with me? "Although you just made a guess about fingerprints, in the end, those guesses were just casual talk. In other words, you never considered that the crime involved his own part.

“…… Maybe it's because of the [Light World Fingerprint]? Maybe it's because of [your] [heart-shaped object]? Maybe it's the ultimate reward from the game system? "Obviously, he wasn't very clear either, so all three in a row were written in a questioning tone. Even so, he probably knew exactly what kind of deal he had made with the game system.

You shrugged: "Fingerprints and stuff are just my guesses...... But if you say that, maybe something like this really will happen in the future. Looks like I can only make that wish." At the end, you muttered softly. Spamton didn't catch it clearly, but he didn't ask you further. He just glanced at you again, and you winked at him innocently.

"Alright, although you are probably right, I still want to know, how exactly do you know that drug was administered by you?" You're very curious about this.

At this moment, Spamton hesitated for a moment, and you couldn't help but touch his hair. It felt a bit restless, and you thought to yourself that you should eat something better later. At that moment, Spamton finally spoke: "...... Because I have some [past record checks]. I [check records] and learned about my [drug administration behavior]. ”

His words might be hard to interpret for police officers and recorders...... After all, translating Spamton's words into normal speech is no easy task. You can have a normal conversation with him, but in rare cases, his words can only give you a partial understanding.

So now you frowned and asked, "...... So, do you mean you have previous memories? ”

But Spamton just shook his head.

Alright, now you more or less understand what he means, so you nod and say with some understanding, "I see. So, in fact, you only have records of past behaviors...... In other words, it's kind of like a game record? ”

At this moment, Spamton nodded: "Not a bit off." ”

Once you figure out the real reason, you're quite happy, but then even more questions flood in, like ......

You frowned, puzzled: "But to you, why does all this feel like a game?" "No matter how you look at it, the world of light is just a real world—it's your real world. If it's fake...... Then the light world created by that game system is even more fake."

At this moment, Spamton was just silent for a moment. He actually had a guess, but wasn't sure if he should say it out loud. And you anxiously shook him: "Go ahead and say it, okay? Judging by your expression, it's obvious you know something, right? ”

The little puppet was rocked back and forth by you, and he was a bit dizzy too. In the end, he couldn't help but give you an answer: "I suspect that someone else [used] my [Body Control Permission] before. In other words, for that person, I was only part of the [game system], and because of this, there was [drug administration]." ”

You blink, unable to believe what you've just heard...... What if your consciousness only arises under someone else's control? You shudder, but then quickly shake your head to deny that view. At the end of the day, you're still an atheist—although atheists shouldn't believe in game systems, ghosts, magic, or illusions. But you firmly believe that your path is a future freely chosen by yourself, and the meaning of your life is interpreted by yourself.

So you just pinch the puppet's little face: "Then, maybe this world is just a game world to others...... But for me, this is the real future, my future, even though it's just a possibility that hasn't happened yet. ”

If this world is just a game to others, or even just a piece of text, or an abandoned field...... That's okay, because for you, only the reality before you matters. Given that, maybe there's no need to be so obsessed with why Spamton appeared in the world of light. After all...... There are many possibilities, and you can't verify them all—you don't have the time.

"So...... Alright, I think maybe I did those things to cover up the fact that you put medicine into your food. After all, I still hope you're okay, at least not dragged into it by me. "It's just ...... Now you can only stay in this detention cell. In the end, your plan still didn't work. I'm afraid Spamton was present at the time.

And you watch Spamton turn his head and give you a look: "...... No, this probably isn't the complete [Accident Scene Incident]. "He was so certain.

But when you glance at the screen and see the progress is still at 50%, not moving, you can't help but feel a bit worried.

"Alright...... But besides that, I can't think of any other motives. Unless someone else is involved. Wait, the others...... "You frowned slightly. You remembered—after all, you're the eldest, with two younger sisters and of course the youngest brother.

Spamton then added, "Today happens to be the seventh day after the New Year." ”

You blink sluggishly, then widen your eyes—you probably understand: "So it's only been two or three days since they arrested us...... Before that, they hadn't caught us." "So, going to the crime scene and catching you was actually a lie, or a kind of test for you? You don't know what exactly they were trying to do. Now it can be perfectly proven that you really have no memory of that part of the incident...... But what else?

"Yes, this is probably the [First Truth]." You saw the little puppet nod, and then you saw the progress bar on the screen jump up by 5%.

You scratched your head, feeling embarrassed, so you lowered your head and whispered, "...... If that's the case, then when I was caught, I probably dragged you into it too. Sorry, you must have been dragged into it by me." ”

But Spamton simply shook his head: "I don't think [you] have any [necessity] to apologize. If [you] lose me, I'm afraid there will be no [chance of victory] in this [crime investigation game]." ”

His words were a bit heavy to you, but you knew most of what he said was true. Still, you couldn't help but mutter, "Even though I might only get caught, at least I can find a way to escape...... It's not like it's impossible. ”

You hear Spamton sigh and gently stroke your arm twice: "...... No, it's my [wrong words and actions], I am deeply sorry to you, and at the same time, I suspect, [someone: framing]. ”

Now you scratch your head, feeling a bit embarrassed. To be honest, you never expected him to apologize, since you weren't blaming him: "...... No need to apologize to me, I'm not blaming you. We don't need to be so cautious, right? If we really do something wrong, I'll tell you. So if I do something wrong, tell me too." You gently shook the puppet's small hand, taking a moment to realize the second part of the sentence.

"I used to guess if someone was framing me...... But after thinking for a long time, I couldn't figure out who would do such a thing. If it's New Year, probably all of us will go home. If not us, maybe everyone else will be suspicious. ”

As you spoke, you lowered your head and glanced at Spamton, a bit helpless: "Besides...... I don't even know why you were taking the medicine. Is it because of me? ”

Spamton nodded: "After all, I don't want my [Angel] to suffer [rude restraints]." "That's what he said.

You scratched your head, feeling a bit embarrassed hearing this, and cleared your throat twice: "Not really...... Hmm, but I really don't like them much, do I?" "By the end, your voice quieted. That's also why you don't really like that place, and don't even consider it your own home."

You all quieted down. Guessing any more at this point wouldn't yield much results. Better to wait until tomorrow and see what happened. It wasn't too early, and dinner was brought over. It was simple, just grilled sausages and bread. And no matter how you looked at it, it was for one person.

You and Spamton shared dinner, then you lay on the somewhat hard and damp bed, tossing and turning several times, frowning, and finally managed to fall asleep.

But the next day, before you could wake up, you were pulled up. You hadn't even had breakfast yet, and the female officer who came to escort you to the interrogation room coldly said she would interrogate you first.

You nervously swallowed twice and sat in the same seat as yesterday. The other side was empty, and you waited hungry for quite a while, not knowing how long. In short, just as you couldn't help but wonder if this was a form of torture for you, you heard a crisp sound from the door, and then saw the police come in with the recorder and sit across from you.

Originally bored and undisciplined, you quickly sat up straight. You were waiting for the police to question you today, since you couldn't say how to make up this story to make them believe everything you said was true.

But this time, the police didn't interrogate you first. Instead, they took out a plastic bag containing evidence and placed it on the table, then quietly watched you. You couldn't help but swallow twice and looked down to see it was indeed a pair of gloves—still stained with blood, seemingly from when you wore the gloves and held a kitchen knife while attacking.

But these gloves didn't leave any water stains on them, which was completely opposite to the excuse you casually made yesterday. Now you were somewhat annoyed with these police. They weren't showing evidence all at once; instead, they were conducting it in stages. What exactly are they trying to do, gradually breaking down your psychological defenses and forcing you to tell the truth?

You swallowed hard, looked the officer straight in the eye, and smiled: "...... Yes, I lied yesterday, I admit it. "Even though you don't want to admit it at all.

But the officer just nodded, then suddenly slammed the table, startling you and helping you refocus your previously distracted attention. You stared at the officer, but after slamming the table, he acted as if nothing had happened and said slowly, "Then, could you please repeat your testimony again?" ”

You took a deep breath. With the experience from last time, you were quick to explain, but you didn't want to expose Spamton, so naturally, it all involved you: "I returned home, and out of resentment and other emotions, I put potassium cyanide in my parents' meals. I wanted them to get what they deserved, so I was willing to turn myself in." But later, when another argument broke out at the dinner table, I still had some sanity and knew to put on gloves and commit the crime. But after I took them off, I didn't have time to deal with them before being arrested by the arriving police. ”

The officer nodded but said, "We only arrested you three days after receiving the report and discovering the scene." ”

You look up in disbelief, because the lie you just made up is now being exposed again—even though you still thought it should be flawless.

So you take another deep breath and ask a different question this time: "So...... Were there surveillance cameras nearby at the time? "If there were, maybe something was recorded to prove it was all framed...... Even though you didn't have much hope.

The officer nodded but shook his head: "There are no surveillance cameras near your house, but there are cameras on a street farther away, about a hundred meters from your house. The surveillance camera captured your two sisters fleeing and you fleeing with Spamton." ”

So that's why you and Spamton were arrested together...... Logically, he shouldn't have been discovered, but since his fingerprints were on the gloves, it was probably normal for him to be arrested as one of the suspects.

You didn't ask the question, but the police seemed to know what you wanted to ask and spoke first: "Aren't you curious whose fingerprints are inside the gloves?" ”

You suddenly lift your head and stare at the officer, who just says slowly, "That glove contains your and Mr. Spamton's fingerprints, and the bloodstains on the glove are from Mr. X and Mr. Y, respectively." ”

…… This evidence is conclusive, and in theory, there should be no room for you to refute it, or in other words, you have no need to argue, yet they still insist on questioning you separately.

You first raised a possibility: "Maybe someone wore another layer of gloves and then put them on to commit the crime?" ”

The police snorted coldly, "So, who are you suspecting?" ”

You felt unusually nervous, perhaps because of the environment, since everything here felt oppressive and gloomy.

“…… Mine, my two sisters, and that one younger brother." You calmly stated the person you suspected. You knew very well that if you left the family, the remaining two sisters would become targets of oppression...... In other words, they might suddenly rise up and commit crimes. Even though you don't want to frame others to clear your name, ...... You can't take the blame for the real culprit for no reason.

But the officer nodded in satisfaction: "We suspect these people as well. Because the remaining evidence is so perfect, it can verify your crimes, which actually feels unreal." ”

He said this, but it made you widen your eyes—you never expected it to be like this.

Meanwhile, Spamton watched as you were taken away. He was a bit worried about your condition, but for now, he couldn't go anywhere else. Even if he was anxious, there was nothing he could do. Soon after, another police officer arrived to take him away, also appearing to be a female officer...... In other words, he really was mistaken for a woman. Thinking about this, Spamton couldn't help but feel a bit conflicted.

But he couldn't think further. The officer led him to sit in the same chair in the interrogation room. Considering his height, the chair was relatively low, but once he sat down, he could adjust the height himself—or rather, after climbing up, since if he didn't adjust the height, only a tuft of hair on his head would be visible above the table.

After adjusting the height, he propped his chin up on the table, pondering what kind of testimony he should fabricate later—to be honest, he hadn't really figured it out, but now the police had already brought the recorder into the room.

So Spamton decided to strike first. He cleared his throat and said, "Miss Y/N and I are in a [boyfriend and girlfriend relationship]. After she [went home to visit relatives and friends], and because I knew her [years of experiences], I decided to secretly [administer drugs: poisoning], and thus added [drugs: potassium cyanide] to the food. At the same time, after that, because of Y/N's insulting behavior, I was [furious], so I used a kitchen knife to carry out the [execution procedure]. ”

The police officer opposite nodded, while the recorder was quickly writing something, though perhaps it wasn't very important. Spamton watched as the officer took out another evidence bag and placed it on the table. If he wasn't mistaken...... Inside that evidence bag was something he had never seen before—a leftover piece of cloth. Of course, it definitely wasn't something on him, but it wasn't something on you either.

The officer said slowly, "We re-examined the scene and found this piece of cloth stained with two types of blood in a crack at the scene. According to the investigation, this piece of cloth belonged to Miss Y/N's younger brother, Mr. Z. ”

Spamton stared intently at the cloth block, now realizing that both you and he had been deceived by the police, and he hadn't noticed it right away—well, if the evidence was so solid, why interrogate them? That meant there were still doubts about the case.

He took a deep breath but simply said, "But officer, this doesn't mean much." "Yes, even though he knows you also hate your brother—after all, if he weren't here, your family might not have pushed you so hard. It's precisely because of him that your parents keep urging you to pay for their support. But even without him, there might still be others, and your parents won't give up.

What he said now was not covering for him, but hoping the police would reveal more information.

As expected, the officer nodded: "Yes, if that alone doesn't prove anything, but when we were investigating at his home, we found records of potassium cyanide purchases." ”

This statement made Spamton's eyes widen—he didn't believe his game records could be faked. In his memory, he did indeed add potassium cyanide to the food, but maybe the medicine in the bottle had been replaced at the time? He couldn't find any earlier memories, but Spamton knew very well that if that was the case, maybe he had made some kind of deal with that Z family. In other words, the medicine he put in the meal wasn't potassium cyanide, but a secretly replaced drug.

“…… In that case, what is your purpose of interrogation? Y/N's actions are to cover for me, because I committed a serious crime because of Y/N. I am here to [turn myself in]. As he spoke, Spamton opened the game's light screen to take a look. On the screen, the current progress of side quests was displayed, somehow turning into 65%. He had a feeling that perhaps this time, side quests would be completed together with the main quest.

He thought, perhaps through these repeated interrogations, you will get closer to the truth.

The officer nodded: "I know. Maybe you're covering for each other—or maybe you're covering for the real culprit. Unfortunately, we don't have enough evidence right now to prove he committed the crime, so we need your help." The officer stood up and slammed his hand heavily on the table. Unfortunately, Spamton wouldn't react differently because of it. He had no memory left from earlier, and everything could only be speculated upon—so at this moment, he was actually a bit troubled. Perhaps this was the real tricky part of this game world.

After saying this, the officer didn't say anything else. He simply waved his hand, took out his walkie-talkie, and said something. Then a police officer came in, bringing Spamton, who had both hands shackled, back into the detention room. This time, he saw you sitting on the lower bunk, looking rather gloomy. But when you saw him, your eyes truly lit up, and you bent down to hold him back in your arms.

You muttered, "Now I actually think this side quest is much easier to complete than the main quest...... Honestly, I have absolutely no idea how to get someone to clear their name and then leave this place. ”

Spamton simply knelt on your lap, reached out his puppet's small hand to smooth your furrowed brows: "Don't [worry about anxiety]. I believe that once they learn the [truth of the real incident], they will [release] both you and me." ”

You sighed: "...... Yeah, I know, but I don't know how long we'll be spending here. From what we see now, besides me, the other three family members are also suspects......

But before you could finish, Spamton interrupted: "No, maybe only your brother, Mr. Z, is a suspect." ”

You blinked. To be honest, the police questioning you didn't tell you more. They only had someone bring you back after you raised suspicions of three others and answered their questions.

“…… Alright, then tell me, what exactly is his suspicion? "You really don't know anything.

Spamton hesitated before saying, "His [shopping list] includes a record for [Drug Purchase: Potassium Cyanide]. Therefore, I should have reached a [specific transaction] with him, so that during the [drug dispensing process] in the meal, [Drug Substitution] was carried out. ”

As he spoke, you even turned on the game screen to check the progress. Now the progress is at 75%. You never really understood how these numbers were calculated...... In short, it must be a good thing, right?

You rub your aching temples, a bit troubled: "Could it be that then he realized his parents didn't die from the poison as he hoped, so he took out another pair of gloves and put on some kind of gloves to start a bloody massacre?" "After spending so much time with Spamton, you're almost learning how to speak him. Luckily, you just managed to change your mind."

The numbers on the game's light screen didn't fluctuate, which means your guess was probably wrong this time...... You sighed, but felt it was only natural. You didn't think things could end so easily.

Spamton nodded but then shook his head: "Then why is there [My fingerprint record] on the gloves?" That's the [suspicious point]. ”

You couldn't help but nod: "Indeed...... Well, this really is a problem that doesn't make sense. We don't remember anything now, and there's nothing we can do anymore. Let's hope the police can help." You sighed as you spoke.

"If we had [Past Saves], then our [Thrusters] wouldn't be needed for side quests." Spamton tugged her skirt and pinched your face, finally snapping you back to reality.

You hugged Spamton and lay back on the bed: "Yeah...... Ah, I really hate this pattern! ”

"No, besides that, we can also make [conceptual speculations]; it's not like we're [accomplishing nothing]." he said.

And you pursed your lips: "Even though you say that, it's not easy to do it either...... Besides, I can't deduce anything from those pieces of evidence myself. ”

If the police hadn't said that this detention wouldn't leave any criminal records, you wouldn't have been willing to cooperate—well, after all, it's a matter closely related to your own life, so it's better to pay more attention. Thinking this, you can't help but sigh again.

"On the bright side, maybe next time you try, you'll be able to leave the police station." Spamton said as he lifted his body to smooth your furrowed brows. Although the puppet's hand was just a little bigger, the slightly chilly sensation did help ease your restless mood a little.

You just sighed, "Alright...... I hope so, as long as there are no other problems. "Besides, this is the last game, and you don't want any surprises—even though for now, you think you can see the light of the ending without much effort. You even suspect that the purpose of this game is to let you know what kind of future you would have if nothing changed. Indeed, you shudder at the possibility.

At the same time, you have firmly believed in how exactly you should make wishes to the game system. Even if the game system can't grant your wish, you can rely on other ways to barely achieve it, though it might be much more troublesome. But if the game system can fulfill your wish, then everything is truly perfect.

Spamton just nodded: "I don't think any other accidents will happen." "For some reason, he had a feeling that maybe everything would end during the next interrogation."

And you just sighed again, not having high expectations for this matter: "It's better to fear just the worst...... You can't really say what might happen, right? ”

At this moment, Spamton fell silent. After all, he truly didn't know what else might happen. Maybe his premonition was wrong, since in his last desperate attempt, he hadn't succeeded either—but when he saw you, he vaguely felt that even if nothing succeeded, it didn't matter. Maybe there were other opportunities, not just those small, even unrealistic expectations.

…… Maybe he could try the Heavenly Wishing Machine, the ultimate wish of the game system, but this time, it was still a gamble—he didn't know if it would succeed. But aside from that, he didn't seem to have any other way—he didn't want to use you when you knew nothing, because Spamton also knew you would absolutely, absolutely hate that. But if you confessed everything, you would never agree to his plan. After spending so much time together, he already understood what you were thinking.

As long as he didn't reveal everything, all of this remained just a secret related to him, nothing more.

You all just sat in silence, falling into an awkward silence. You felt ashamed of it and thought maybe we could start a different topic—luckily, the food arrived just now, and you finally breathed a sigh of relief. Fortunately, you didn't have to keep thinking about how to break the kind of silence. Wasn't this just right now?

Just then, your stomach growled twice, and you coughed twice to break the silence. Although the meals in the detention room were simple, they were still better than the meals you had in your previous rented apartment. Unlike back then, when you ate instant noodles every day, the current meals were still proper bacon omelets and two slices of bread, but the meals weren't really unfair to you.

You pick up a slice of bread and hand it to Spamton, then pick up another slice and pop it into your mouth. Although the meal at this time is for two people, you still doubt whether it's really enough. Spamton doesn't seem to have any doubts, but quickly finishes the slice of bread, just like every time they eat.

You always worry he might burn himself or something, but the truth proves you were probably overthinking it. Even when we ate hotpot before, he never changed his expression. You know very well that the meat slices we gave him were piping hot, and you don't know how much his taste buds had been ruined by soaking in the acid bath before...... And if it were the burning hot taste, it should have caused him a secondary injury, but he showed no other reaction, as if he was already used to all this, or maybe ...... Was he just forcing himself to endure? Although you quickly realized you had fed and rubbed it, and from then on you would blow it cool before feeding Spamton.

You placed him at your side, the tray on your lap, turned your head to stare at him for a while, and Spamton, after gnawing on the bread, looked up at you in confusion—though you didn't know how you could read his mood through the lens, maybe it was just a feeling. Yes, a feeling.

You cleared your throat and said cautiously—well, maybe you were just straightforward: "Ahem, I'm a bit curious. The first time I took you out for hotpot, I fed you a piece of steaming hot meat. Do you really think it's okay?" "You know this has nothing to do with the current situation, and you're just recalling the scene back then, lost in your thoughts."

Actually, if you hadn't said anything, Spamton would have almost forgotten about it. He was stunned for a moment, hesitated for a moment, and finally remembered what had happened back then. So he explained seriously to you: "...... No, it's indeed very related, it's [hot!] Scalding! It's unbearable! ], but...... Although I don't want to [experience] a similar [feeling] again. I'm still [used to it]. "At this point, he was a bit puzzled...... Why did he take it for granted? Actually, he had never felt this way before; he just subconsciously deceived you. If he told you his true feelings and psychological experiences, you would definitely feel guilty. Moreover, you quickly realize something was wrong, and even cooling it first before feeding it, which only makes him feel more embarrassed.

After all, accepting someone's pure kindness always makes you feel ashamed. He is also unwilling to accept your kindness for no reason...... No, maybe your fate has long been tangled in these processes.

You blinked. Although watching Spamton say those words without changing his composure eased your heart, you couldn't help but complain, "...... Don't get used to things that hurt you. When it hurts, you should speak up. Someone will always stand up for you, and besides, I'm here now—I definitely won't let you get hurt again." As you spoke, you patted your chest. Although you felt a little guilty saying that, you quickly pulled yourself together. Everything you said was true—you genuinely wanted to protect him.

Spamton looked up at you, quickly, then lowered his head, his voice muffled: "...... No, at that moment, even if I sent a casualty rescue signal, no one would have come to the rescue operation." He knew this very well.

After all...... After becoming a big shot, he truly enjoyed a carefree life for quite a while, but he also lost his old friends. Those Addisons—or perhaps it was they who didn't want to be friends anymore. After all, he really didn't need friends at the time—he was more concerned about the issue of putting interests above all. In the end, he became completely empty-handed, but no Dark People ever came looking for him. Only then did he realize that friendship was truly broken and could never be maintained.

“…… But now it's different. If you really get hurt, please tell me. After all, we're not just partners, but also ...... Uh, boyfriend and girlfriend. "Your hands slipped over his arms and picked him up, speaking to him enthusiastically. But when you looked him in the eye, you got stuck again halfway through the sentence, your face flushed unnaturally, and in the end, you had to turn your head away in a hurry. Belatedly, you realized you would really feel embarrassed when you said that.

Spamton straightened his face and stared straight at you, but before he could say anything else, the female officer who had taken you to the interrogation room came over again. You instinctively stood up, thinking, Didn't there already be a round of interrogation this morning? You thought Spamton was here chatting because it was all free time!

Thinking this, you couldn't help but ask again. Even though you obediently followed behind when the female officer opened the cage: "Didn't you already collect your confession this morning?" "Anyway, unless all the records from the morning were destroyed or something...... But that's impossible.

The female officer closed the door to the detention cell. Spamton instinctively climbed onto the windowsill to watch your figure fade into the distance, realizing that something had changed—completely different from what he had expected.

But she just gave you a cold look: "That statement is now void because new evidence has emerged to identify you and has also overturned the previous testimony." ”

You blink, racking your brains but still can't figure out what evidence could overturn all the testimonies and get the police to rush you to collect your testimony right now. Yes, if you're not mistaken, just as you were turning a corner, you saw a police officer bring Spamton out of the detention cell. Although to this day we still don't know why the police station ruled Spamton a woman...... Uh, maybe the Dark People don't actually have gender distinctions?

Lost in thought, you soon arrived at the interrogation room. You sat down in your usual seat, everything just like when you left that morning, except that a police officer and recorder had switched across from you. You instinctively became alert.

“…… What about that officer from before? "In short, you still brought up this question, and you think it's harmless.

But the officer just impatiently tapped the table with his knuckles: "This has nothing to do with you, you don't need to know. Please don't talk nonsense about cases that don't matter; just provide testimony related to the case." As he spoke, his expression serious and his tone icy cold, so cold you almost shivered—but not really.

You just feel that this officer's attitude is different from the two times he questioned you, so you tentatively ask: "...... The previous officer was transferred from this case? He's not responsible for this case anymore? ”

Visibly, the officer's body stiffened for a moment. Perhaps the question was indeed true—in short, he didn't answer your question, but spoke rapidly: "Please don't talk about things unrelated to the case." ”

You shrank your neck. Honestly, you're a bit scared. If they really do try to lynch you, there's nothing you can do.

But...... Even if you ask me to speak, you can't say anything useful.

So you throw out your question, which is your last one: "So...... What is the new evidence? The officer who brought me here just now said there is new evidence. ”

Anyway, you don't have any memories of before, so you can only take it one step at a time to see what evidence you have......

Then you see the police show you an evidence bag, which also contains a pair of rubber gloves. Compared to the previous one, this pair fits your hand more closely and looks even tighter.

You swallowed hard, and before you could say anything else, the officer said, "On the outer surface of these gloves, we collected your fingerprints, Mr. X, Mr. Y, and Mr. Z, as well as Mr. Spamton's, but inside the gloves, only your fingerprints were collected. We reasonably suspect you used those gloves and put on another pair to commit murder. ”

You twitched the corner of your mouth, feeling that there must be something odd about this evidence, but you couldn't bring it up—in the end, you could only sigh: "Alright, since that's the case, this doesn't completely overturn my previous testimony. You started muttering this, starting to worry. Spamton was silent for a moment, then touched his own pocket. Though he said it was a pocket, only the pocket of his jacket was worth looking at, and he really did pull out a driver's license from inside.

His name was still clearly written on it: Spamton G. Spamton.

Not only that, but there were even birth dates, issuance dates, and resident numbers. You glanced at the documents, and sure enough, they weren't fake. You stroked your chin and nodded, "Alright, then there's no problem...... But it's too late today. Tomorrow we'll go check out the house and rent it. Since there's nothing to move in anyway, let's just go on a big shopping spree! ”

Besides, you still have to attend class first, so you don't have time to deal with anything else. Fortunately, the teacher just thought Spamton was a doll or something and didn't interfere. You took Spamton to class all night, and he was so frustrated pretending to be a well-behaved doll in your arms.

After class, you took Spamton back to the Dark World. You were already very tired, and after a quick wash and tidying up, you hugged Spamton and lay down on the bed. Half-awake, you heard Spamton in his arms ask you a question.

"If you didn't have the [Heaven Wishing Machine], what would you do?"

You think he's just asking what he already knows, but you still yawn and force yourself to answer: "What's there to talk about...... As for me, I was probably just struggling in society, working hard to earn some money, and then one day I couldn't help but fall out with my family. That's all there is to it." "But you probably wouldn't kill anyone...... So, it was probably just your brother framing you, but you actually don't have any hope of surviving yourself.

Or rather, for you, there was no real meaning in survival...... But now, you're willing to walk with Spamton. If one day you find such days dull, then just deal with it then. But you think Spamton probably occasionally gives you some fright, so you probably won't ever feel bored.

After saying this, you hugged Spamton and drifted into a deep sleep. Once again, you dreamed of Spamton as a salesman, dressed in a red suit, looking radiant, and he was talking to you. You knew he was talking about wanting to see your soul. Of course, you couldn't show it to him—but in the dream, you were driven by a daze, driving your soul and body apart. It was a red heart, still faintly radiating some light.

Spamton seemed somewhat captivated, then he looked up and smiled at you: "Miss, your heart-shaped object is truly [beautiful and unique]. I think I already [love] you." ”

You instinctively step back, remembering that maybe this was the conversation you first met, but after that, you tacitly put everything out of your mind—after all, everything was different from now. Your soul trembled, as if stirred by those words, since you had never experienced anything like this before. So you fled in panic, just throwing away money in hopes of washing away this inexplicable memory.

And indeed, you must hurry up, since no one has mentioned it again. But your actions back then may have been a silent prayer in your heart, hoping that one day there will be a new connection between you. Mutual debt is always a form of connection and bondage.

And now, everything is over. You don't want to worry about the truth of those words, but after waking up, you're still a bit sleepy, and then you kiss Spamton on the forehead.

He had come to his senses at this moment, probably hesitating for a moment, then tilted his head and kissed your lips.

You stretched and sat up, finally fully awake. You changed your clothes with full energy, then took Spamton out for breakfast, met with the landlord of your rental apartment, successfully moved out, and then headed to the world of light.

You looked for a place near the school, viewed several units, and finally decided to rent one of the well-decorated ones that wasn't too expensive. Although it was a one-bedroom, you and Spamton both slept together, so of course it was fine.

And for some reason, landlords and other Lightfolk only treat Spamton as a doll. Even if the doll moves and talks, there's no reaction from them. You might wonder if Lightfolk's acceptance is a bit too high.

But Spamton didn't react much. In short, you rented a new big house, but it was empty, with nothing at all, so you just slung Spamton over your shoulder and went shopping.

First up are mattresses and similar items—mattresses—in short, a whole range of bedding items you need to buy. You can boldly wash bed sheets and duvet covers and then dry them, so they're ready to use right away. Then you start adding daily necessities, like pots and pans, and regular toiletries. Once you get these, you'll have some everyday clothes and items you might use but don't use often...... And kitchen supplies.

You pick up a bucket of oil, look at the label and put it in your cart, then look at Spamton on your shoulder, and sincerely ask, "Spamton, can you cook?" ”

Spamton hesitated for a moment but still nodded: "[Yes]?" But I can't guarantee that the [dish flavors] will match [your dining taste]. ”

You scratched your head: "It's fine." I'm not very particular about taste, I eat whatever you make...... Ah, right, but is there a bit of a problem with the seasoning? Is your taste bud good? ”

"[Theoretically], I [achieve the process of self-repair of taste]." At least when dining out with you, Spamton no longer felt numb to his taste. At first, he found it hard to believe—but later on, he got used to it.

To be honest, he was somewhat grateful for the game system. After all, without it, he wouldn't have met you, nor would he have encountered the present future. Although the game system clearly didn't intend to get you to clear the final level—you had already changed, so it didn't succeed. In the end, your wishes still need to be fulfilled.

You breathed a sigh of relief and nodded, "Alright, then I'll leave the cooking to you—not that I can't cook, I just want to taste what it's like to be served occasionally." "You said this with confidence, Spamton sighed, but in the end, he was instructing you to buy the vegetables he needed and such.

Besides that, you bought some instant food in case you don't have time one day, so you'll have something to eat later.

After bringing all these things home, the small room now had a warmer atmosphere. You carried Spamton out again, this time to sweep the clothing store.

"By the way, what clothes do you want to buy?" If he has no objections, you can buy him some children's clothes for little girls, usually princess dresses or such. The sizes are actually a bit larger and definitely suit him well.

For some reason, Spamton felt a chill down his spine, so he hurriedly said, "I hope it's a [Classic Suit]." "If he heard correctly, you must have sighed in great disappointment!"

But in the end, you still secretly bought him a few children's princess dresses and didn't let him see them. Anyway, you can quietly put them on later! You just want to look, there's nothing wrong with that, you thought righteously.

After a busy schedule, you finally lay down on that big bed, holding Spamton and staring at the ceiling, the corners of your mouth involuntarily lifting.

After all, a brand new, unknown new life was about to begin.

Sunday· END
